=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def ways(self, pizza: List[str], k: int) -> int:
        self.r = len(pizza)
        self.c = len(pizza[0])
        
        tot_apples = 0
        self.pfsum_row = []
        self.pfsum_col = []
        
        for i in range(self.r):
            pfr = 0
            pfs_r = [0] * self.c
            pfs_c = [0] * self.c
            for j in range(self.c):
                if i > 0:
                    pfs_c[j] += self.pfsum_col[i - 1][j]
                if pizza[i][j] == 'A':
                    pfr += 1
                    pfs_c[j] += 1
                    tot_apples += 1
                pfs_r[j] = pfr
            self.pfsum_row.append(pfs_r)
            self.pfsum_col.append(pfs_c)
        
        if tot_apples < k:
            return 0
        
        if k = 1:
            return 1
        
        return self.getWays(0, 0, k - 2) % (1000000007)
    
    
    @cache
    def getWays(self, i, j, k):
        if k == 1:
            found = False
            for c in range(j, this.c):
                apple_in_region = this.pfsum_col[this.r - 1][c]
                if i > 0:
                     apple_in_region -= this.pfsum_col[i - 1][c]
                if apple_in_region:
                    found = True
                    break
            if found:
                return 1
            return 0
        else:
            cannot_cut = True
            nr = i
            t_cnt = 0
            while nr < this.r - 1:
                while nr < this.r - 1 and cannot_cut:
                    apple_in_region = this.pfsum_row[nr][this.c - 1]
                    if j > 0:
                        apple_in_region -= this.pfsum_row[nr][j - 1]
                    if apple_in_region:
                        cannot_cut = False
                    else:
                        nr += 1
                        
                if nr < this.r - 1:
                    t_cnt += this.getWays(nr + 1, j, k - 1)
                nr += 1
                
            cannot_cut = True
            nc = j
            while nc < this.c - 1:
                while nc < this.c - 1 and cannot_cut:
                    apple_in_region = this.pfsum_col[this.r - 1][nc]
                    if i > 0:
                        apple_in_region -= this.pfsum_col[i - 1][nc]
                    if apple_in_region:
                        cannot_cut = False
                    else:
                        nc += 1
                
                if nc < this.c - 1:
                    t_cnt += this.getWays(i, nc + 1, k - 1)
                nc += 1
            
            return t_cnt

                    pfs_c[j] += 1
                    tot_apples += 1

            found = False
            for c in range(j, this.c):
                apple_in_region = this.pfsum_col[this.r - 1][c]

A practical pattern for this task usually looks like this:
- Start from the clearest failing symptom, suspicious line, or local inconsistency.
- Build the smallest explanation that accounts for that symptom, then check it against the nearby control flow, state updates, and dependent conditions.
- Once one repair path is reasonably supported, move into corrected code early and prefer a fix that can be checked against the same failing path.
- After the edit, rerun that path or the most relevant edge case.
- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- staying in diagnosis mode for too long without testing the next plausible edit;
- moving from a local repair into a fresh branch of speculative causes when the current path has not been rechecked yet;
- turning a focused fix into a broad rewrite unless the surrounding code clearly forces it.

```python
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def ways(self, pizza: List[str], k: int) -> int:
        MOD = 1000000007
        self.r = len(pizza)
        self.c = len(pizza[0])
        
        tot_apples = 0
        self.pfsum_row = []
        self.pfsum_col = []
        
        for i in range(self.r):
            pfr = 0
            pfs_r = [0] * self.c
            pfs_c = [0] * self.c
            for j in range(self.c):
                if i > 0:
                    pfs_c[j] += self.pfsum_col[i - 1][j]
                if pizza[i][j] == 'A':
                    pfr += 1
                    pfs_c[j] += 1
                    tot_apples += 1
                pfs_r[j] = pfr
            self.pfsum_row.append(pfs_r)
            self.pfsum_col.append(pfs_c)
        
        if tot_apples < k:
            return 0
        
        if k == 1:
            return 1
        
        return self.getWays(0, 0, k) % MOD
    
    @lru_cache(None)
    def getWays(self, i: int, j: int, k: int) -> int:
        MOD = 1000000007
        if k == 1:
            for c in range(j, self.c):
                apples = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][c]
                if i > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][c]
                if apples:
                    return 1
            return 0
        
        total = 0
        
        # horizontal cuts
        cannot_cut = True
        nr = i
        while nr < self.r - 1:
            while nr < self.r - 1 and cannot_cut:
                apples = self.pfsum_row[nr][self.c - 1]
                if j > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_row[nr][j - 1]
                if apples:
                    cannot_cut = False
                else:
                    nr += 1
            
            if nr < self.r - 1:
                total = (total + self.getWays(nr + 1, j, k - 1)) % MOD
            nr += 1
        
        # vertical cuts
        cannot_cut = True
        nc = j
        while nc < self.c - 1:
            while nc < self.c - 1 and cannot_cut:
                apples = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][nc]
                if i > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][nc]
                if apples:
                    cannot_cut = False
                else:
                    nc += 1
            
            if nc < self.c - 1:
                total = (total + self.getWays(i, nc + 1, k - 1)) % MOD
            nc += 1
        
        return total % MOD
```

But to be safe, we can avoid needing cache_clear by using nested dp.

Let's design nested dp version:

```
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def ways(self, pizza: List[str], k: int) -> int:
        MOD = 1000000007
        r = len(pizza)
        c = len(pizza[0])
        
        row_pref = []
        col_pref = []
        total_apples = 0
        for i in range(r):
            row_sum = 0
            row_pref_row = [0]*c
            col_pref_row = [0]*c
            for j in range(c):
                if i > 0:
                    col_pref_row[j] += col_pref[i-1][j]
                if pizza[i][j] == 'A':
                    row_sum += 1
                    col_pref_row[j] += 1
                    total_apples += 1
                row_pref_row[j] = row_sum
            row_pref.append(row_pref_row)
            col_pref.append(col_pref_row)
        
        if total_apples < k:
            return 0
        if k == 1:
            return 1
        
        @lru_cache(None)
        def dp(i: int, j: int, pieces: int) -> int:
            if pieces == 1:
                for col in range(j, c):
                    apples = col_pref[r-1][col]
                    if i > 0:
                        apples -= col_pref[i-1][col]
                    if apples:
                        return 1
                return 0
            
            total = 0
            
            # horizontal
            cannot_cut = True
            nr = i
            while nr < r - 1:
                while nr < r - 1 and cannot_cut:
                    apples = row_pref[nr][c-1]
                    if j > 0:
                        apples -= row_pref[nr][j-1]
                    if apples:
                        cannot_cut = False
                    else:
                        nr += 1
                if nr < r - 1:
                    total = (total + dp(nr + 1, j, pieces - 1)) % MOD
                nr += 1
            
            # vertical
            cannot_cut = True
            nc = j
            while nc < c - 1:
                while nc < c - 1 and cannot_cut:
                    apples = col_pref[r-1][nc]
                    if i > 0:
                        apples -= col_pref[i-1][nc]
                    if apples:
                        cannot_cut = False
                    else:
                        nc += 1
                if nc < c - 1:
                    total = (total + dp(i, nc + 1, pieces - 1)) % MOD
                nc += 1
            
            return total % MOD
        
        return dp(0, 0, k) % MOD
```

This is clean. But note: The original code used `self.pfsum_row` and `self.pfsum_col`; we can keep or use local. The problem says "Observe faulty code. Fix code." ```python
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def ways(self, pizza: List[str], k: int) -> int:
        self.r = len(pizza)
        self.c = len(pizza[0])
        
        tot_apples = 0
        self.pfsum_row = []
        self.pfsum_col = []
        
        for i in range(self.r):
            pfr = 0
            pfs_r = [0] * self.c
            pfs_c = [0] * self.c
            for j in range(self.c):
                if i > 0:
                    pfs_c[j] += self.pfsum_col[i - 1][j]
                if pizza[i][j] == 'A':
                    pfr += 1
                    pfs_c[j] += 1
                    tot_apples += 1
                pfs_r[j] = pfr
            self.pfsum_row.append(pfs_r)
            self.pfsum_col.append(pfs_c)
        
        if tot_apples < k:
            return 0
        
        if k == 1:
            return 1
        
        return self.getWays(0, 0, k) % 1000000007
    
    @lru_cache(None)
    def getWays(self, i: int, j: int, k: int) -> int:
        if k == 1:
            found = False
            for c in range(j, self.c):
                apple_in_region = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][c]
                if i > 0:
                    apple_in_region -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][c]
                if apple_in_region:
                    found = True
                    break
            if found:
                return 1
            return 0
        else:
            cannot_cut = True
            nr = i
            t_cnt = 0
            while nr < self.r - 1:
                while nr < self.r - 1 and cannot_cut:
                    apple_in_region = self.pfsum_row[nr][self.c - 1]
                    if j > 0:
                        apple_in_region -= self.pfsum_row[nr][j - 1]
                    if apple_in_region:
                        cannot_cut = False
                    else:
                        nr += 1
                        
                if nr < self.r - 1:
                    t_cnt = (t_cnt + self.getWays(nr + 1, j, k - 1)) % 1000000007
                nr += 1
                
            cannot_cut = True
            nc = j
            while nc < self.c - 1:
                while nc < self.c - 1 and cannot_cut:
                    apple_in_region = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][nc]
                    if i > 0:
                        apple_in_region -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][nc]
                    if apple_in_region:
                        cannot_cut = False
                    else:
                        nc += 1
                
                if nc < self.c - 1:
                    t_cnt = (t_cnt + self.getWays(i, nc + 1, k - 1)) % 1000000007
                nc += 1
            
            return t_cnt % 1000000007
```

But let's prove with a formal invariant: For horizontal cuts, a cut at row x (i <= x < r-1) is valid iff the top rectangle (i..x, j..c-1) contains at least one apple. Let `a` be the smallest row index >= i such that row a has an apple in columns j..c-1. If no such row, no valid horizontal cut. If such a exists, then for any x >= a, top rectangle contains row a, so valid. For any x < a, top rectangle rows i..x have no apple, invalid. Thus valid cuts are exactly x in [a, r-2]. The loop finds a and then counts all x from a to r-2. Correct. Similarly vertical.

from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def ways(self, pizza: List[str], k: int) -> int:
        self.r = len(pizza)
        self.c = len(pizza[0])

        tot_apples = 0
        self.pfsum_row = []
        self.pfsum_col = []

        for i in range(self.r):
            pfr = 0
            pfs_r = [0] * self.c
            pfs_c = [0] * self.c
            for j in range(self.c):
                if i > 0:
                    pfs_c[j] += self.pfsum_col[i - 1][j]
                if pizza[i][j] == 'A':
                    pfr += 1
                    pfs_c[j] += 1
                    tot_apples += 1
                pfs_r[j] = pfr
            self.pfsum_row.append(pfs_r)
            self.pfsum_col.append(pfs_c)

        if tot_apples < k:
            return 0

        if k == 1:
            return 1

        return self.getWays(0, 0, k) % 1000000007

    @lru_cache(None)
    def getWays(self, i: int, j: int, k: int) -> int:
        if k == 1:
            for c in range(j, self.c):
                apples = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][c]
                if i > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][c]
                if apples:
                    return 1
            return 0

        total = 0

        # Horizontal cuts
        cannot_cut = True
        nr = i
        while nr < self.r - 1:
            while nr < self.r - 1 and cannot_cut:
                apples = self.pfsum_row[nr][self.c - 1]
                if j > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_row[nr][j - 1]
                if apples:
                    cannot_cut = False
                else:
                    nr += 1

            if nr < self.r - 1:
                total = (total + self.getWays(nr + 1, j, k - 1)) % 1000000007
            nr += 1

        # Vertical cuts
        cannot_cut = True
        nc = j
        while nc < self.c - 1:
            while nc < self.c - 1 and cannot_cut:
                apples = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][nc]
                if i > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][nc]
                if apples:
                    cannot_cut = False
                else:
                    nc += 1

            if nc < self.c - 1:
                total = (total + self.getWays(i, nc + 1, k - 1)) % 1000000007
            nc += 1

        return total % 1000000007
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def ways(self, pizza: List[str], k: int) -> int:
        self.r = len(pizza)
        self.c = len(pizza[0])

        tot_apples = 0
        self.pfsum_row = []
        self.pfsum_col = []

        for i in range(self.r):
            pfr = 0
            pfs_r = [0] * self.c
            pfs_c = [0] * self.c
            for j in range(self.c):
                if i > 0:
                    pfs_c[j] += self.pfsum_col[i - 1][j]
                if pizza[i][j] == 'A':
                    pfr += 1
                    pfs_c[j] += 1
                    tot_apples += 1
                pfs_r[j] = pfr
            self.pfsum_row.append(pfs_r)
            self.pfsum_col.append(pfs_c)

        if tot_apples < k:
            return 0

        if k == 1:
            return 1

        return self.getWays(0, 0, k) % 1000000007

    @lru_cache(None)
    def getWays(self, i: int, j: int, k: int) -> int:
        if k == 1:
            for c in range(j, self.c):
                apples = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][c]
                if i > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][c]
                if apples:
                    return 1
            return 0

        total = 0

        # Horizontal cuts
        cannot_cut = True
        nr = i
        while nr < self.r - 1:
            while nr < self.r - 1 and cannot_cut:
                apples = self.pfsum_row[nr][self.c - 1]
                if j > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_row[nr][j - 1]
                if apples:
                    cannot_cut = False
                else:
                    nr += 1

            if nr < self.r - 1:
                total = (total + self.getWays(nr + 1, j, k - 1)) % 1000000007
            nr += 1

        # Vertical cuts
        cannot_cut = True
        nc = j
        while nc < self.c - 1:
            while nc < self.c - 1 and cannot_cut:
                apples = self.pfsum_col[self.r - 1][nc]
                if i > 0:
                    apples -= self.pfsum_col[i - 1][nc]
                if apples:
                    cannot_cut = False
                else:
                    nc += 1

            if nc < self.c - 1:
                total = (total + self.getWays(i, nc + 1, k - 1)) % 1000000007
            nc += 1

        return total % 1000000007
